## 2026-11-11T02:40Z — Independent review of the admission gate, and fixes

- A separate reviewer agent read the staged diff and found five defects, each confirmed against a test or the record schema. All five are fixed in `src/bin/ic_autolab.rs`:
  1. A `params` entry named `oracle` replaced the admitted oracle in `ArmConfig::method`. Fixed: `ARM_PARAMS` allowlist (`targets` = walk|stride, `linalg`, `max_trials`), and `admit_arm` checks the emitted method.
  2. `pkm-semaev` with a lowered `degree`, `max_nnz` or `staircase` falls back to the grid, which is enumeration. Fixed: the `PKM_PARAMS` allowlist with degree ≥ 12, and `grid_fallback` at assess (more than 1% of calls to the grid, and more than inc, is inadmissible).
  3. The pairing key used `workload.target_counter`, which is a hash retry counter. Fixed: the key is (target_seed, target_index, round), and duplicate keys are refused.
  4. The growth exponent used w = 0 only. Fixed: all three weights, with families recorded and mixed or fewer-than-3-size fits flagged indicative.
  5. `ratchet` did not re-check admission. Fixed.
- `cargo test` on the standalone copy: 19 passed. `rustfmt --check` passes. Clippy shows only the repo-allowed `too_many_arguments` and `type_complexity`.
- `DECISIONS.md` R1 and R2 updated to match.

- `guess` (the hybrid guess-and-determine split) stays admitted: F4 still solves each specialised system. The grid-share rule catches a split that leaves F4 nothing to decide.

## 2026-11-10T18:10Z — F2 launched: linear complexity of the p²-lift defect along the group (Claude, main session)


- Study `research/p2_lift_linear_complexity_20261110/`. `PROTOCOL.md` frozen at `4da4a01f06` (predictions and rules) before the tool existed; amended at `6930d073e2` for the set-S selection rule and the O5 modulus (§11 discloses the implementer's smoke runs, all consistent with the frozen predictions). `FREEZE.sha256` binds protocol, `tool/src/main.rs`, `Cargo.toml`, `Cargo.lock`.
- Design (main session): with `r ∤ p` the lift sequence splits uniquely on `⟨G⟩`, so H110's `τ₀ = r·f` with `f` the correction from the fixed-x section to the canonical torsion lift; every first-layer lift observable is a function of `f` and coordinate functions. The untested structure is translation structure along the group: the linear complexity `L` over `F_p` of `k ↦ f([k]G)` equals the DFT support size over `F_{p^m}`, `m = ord_r(p)` (the embedding degree); a sparse support would transfer the ECDLP to `μ_r ⊂ F_{p^m}^*` through the lift. Gated observables O1 (`f₀`) and O6 (`torsion_u`, the x-digit of the canonical torsion lift); negative control O3 (coordinate-only), 200 random sequences per curve (null), positive controls O5 (`Tr(αζ^k)` on supersingular curves, `L = 2`) and O7 (mean removal); A/A = decimation invariance across generator multipliers.
- Tool: std-only Rust crate written by a Sonnet agent (port of H110 `run.py`; `τ₀`, `τ₁`, `torsion_u` on E101 equal H110's values in a unit test; 7/7 tests pass). Card `cards/F2-RUN-01.json` (experiment-director, lint OK, sha256 `b6aae73c…`), relayed verbatim to executor-mechanical at 18:04Z; `run-full` detached, expected 3–4 h on the 4-core host (R16's Berlekamp–Massey dominates).
